Conclusion: 要点压缩对特定任务类型有益，但对时间性问题有害，原因在于提示词中丢失时间信息的特定机制。通过精确的提示词修改可以恢复这种损失，而不影响其他保留率。这为有效使用要点压缩提供了见解。

Abstract: Gist-based context compression---summarising older conversation history into compact representations---is a common approach in long-horizon language model agents, yet its effect on different types of memory retrieval is poorly understood. We use Salience-Weighted Consolidation (SWC), a biologically-inspired compression framework motivated by sleep-based memory consolidation, as a diagnostic probe to study when gist compression helps and when it hurts. SWC scores conversation history by salience, partitions it into priority tiers, and applies structured gist abstraction to mid-priority content. Evaluating four conditions on all ten LoCoMo conversations---1,935 matched text-only questions in total, 1,501 used in the primary aggregate after excluding Category 5 (adversarial) questions---at temperature 0, we find a consistent task-type interaction: gist compression substantially outperforms truncation on multi-hop reasoning and single-hop factual questions, but temporal questions remain substantially harder under compression, with compressed conditions scoring well below the full-context reference on the conversations where both are evaluated. We trace this failure to a specific mechanism: the gist abstraction prompt preserves relational and event structure while discarding dates and times. A preservation analysis across all ten conversations confirms the mechanism: an approximately 20-fold increase in temporal expression preservation (3.05% to 62.39%) with a one-sentence prompt modification, while named entity and event preservation rates barely change (x1.02 and x1.11), demonstrating that the fix is a precision instrument. The prompt modification recovers +0.314 [0.254, 0.375] judge accuracy on category-2 (temporal) questions in the matched set. Code and results: https://github.com/kyrkewood/sleeping-agent.

</details>


### [241] [Agent Skills Can Be Harmful: An Empirical Study of Skill-Induced Failures in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11888)
*Gen Dong,Yanjie Gao,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Yu Hua,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了LLM智能体技能（skills）导致的任务失败和成本增加问题，提出微分分析框架将失败归因于特定技能，发现表面相关的技能更容易导致错误实现，效率回退不仅由提示长度解释，并提出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能是扩展LLM智能体能力的常用机制，但先前研究显示技能效果不一：部分提升成功率，部分无效甚至增加令牌使用、执行时间或降低成功率。缺乏对技能导致的失败和成本回归的系统性分析和归因方法，阻碍了技能的安全和成本意识复用。

Method: 提出微分分析框架，通过比较目标技能引导的运行与无技能或语义匹配的技能参考运行，将失败或回归归因于特定加载的技能。在SkillsBench和SWE-Skills-Bench上实例化该框架，识别307个技能诱导的失败案例（125个功能失败和182个效率回归）。构建SkillTriage工具，基于分类法归因，标准化配对案例，提取微分证据，生成分类报告。

Result: 主要发现：（1）技能诱导的功能失败很少由明显不相关的技能引起；相反，看似相关的技能常使智能体错误实现或遗漏任务必需的实现元素。（2）技能诱导的效率回归不能仅由提示长度解释。（3）过度程序化中最大的来源是过度验证（67例）和重型实现管线（30例），表明技能常将验证清单和构建配方转化为强制性工作。

Conclusion: 基于发现，提出面向更安全、成本意识更强的技能复用的研究课题和工具改进建议，包括改进技能设计规范、增强归因分析工具、开发效率监控机制等方向。

Abstract: Agent skills are the de facto mechanism for extending LLM agents with reusable guidance. A skill can shape the agent's task execution, including planning, tool use, problem-solving, and validation. Prior work reported mixed results of agent skills: some skills improve task success rates, while others have no effect, increase token use and execution time, and even reduce success rates. This paper presents a comprehensive analysis of skill-induced agent failures by attributing task failures and cost regressions to specific loaded skills. We introduce a differential analysis framework that attributes a failure or regression to a skill by comparing a target skill-guided run against a no-skill or semantically matched skill reference run that solves the same task, or solves it more cheaply. We instantiate this framework on SkillsBench and SWE-Skills-Bench, yielding 307 skill-induced failures, including 125 functional failures and 182 efficiency regressions. We also build SkillTriage, a taxonomy-guided attribution tool that normalizes paired cases, extracts differential evidence, and produces triage reports. Our major findings include: (1) Skill induced functional failures are rarely caused by obviously irrelevant skills; instead, seemingly relevant skills often make the agent incorrectly implement or omit task-required implementation elements. (2) Skill-induced efficiency regressions are not explained by prompt length alone. (3) The largest sources within Excessive Procedure are excessive verification and heavy implementation pipelines, contributing 67 and 30 cases, respectively. This shows that skills often turn validation checklists and construction recipes into mandatory work. Based on our findings, we propose research topics and tooling improvements for safer and more cost-aware skill reuse.

</details>


### [242] [Policy-as-logic for robust reasoning over rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11905)
*Rahul Nair,Bastian Lipka,Elizabeth Daly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合符号方法，将政策表示为形式逻辑，利用语言模型进行事实提取以填充谓词，并使用答案集求解器进行推理，确保生成式AI响应符合书面政策，在大多数情况下优于现有方法且token使用量减少约10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际生成式AI应用（如税务规则、航空公司行李规定）要求自然语言查询的响应必须遵守书面政策或规则，但现有方法（如policy-as-prompt、policy-as-code）可能在合规性、鲁棒性或效率方面存在不足。

Method: 采用混合符号方法：将政策表示为形式逻辑；在推理时使用语言模型提取事实以填充谓词；使用答案集求解器进行逻辑推理。该方法将事实提取与推理步骤分离。

Result: 实验表明该方法在可解释性、可审计性、准确性以及对输入扰动的鲁棒性方面表现良好；在大多数情况下优于policy-as-prompt和policy-as-code方法，token使用量减少约10倍。

Conclusion: 结构化推理与符号求解器结合生成式模型，可为涉及客观标准的稳健决策提供有效方案，展现出在政策合规场景中的显著优势。

Abstract: In many practical applications of generative AI systems, from tax rules to airline baggage allowance, responses to natural language queries must respect written policies or rules. We present a hybrid symbolic approach that expresses policies in formal logic and at inference time exploits the representation power of language models for fact extraction to ground predicates, and an answer set solver for reasoning such that responses are interpretable, auditable, and as we show, accurate and robust under input perturbations. Specifically, we show this separation of extraction and reasoning steps outperforms policy-as-prompt and policy-as-code methods in most cases with ~10x reduction in token usage. The results point to the value of structured reasoning and symbolic solvers in conjunction with generative models to make robust decisions involving objective criteria.

</details>


### [243] [OEIS Open: How many conjectures can language models turn into theorems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11941)
*Tom Adamczewski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We construct OEIS Open, a benchmark based on 492 open mathematical conjectures from the OEIS, formalized in Lean by Tsoukalas et al. Whereas these conjectures had previously been attempted only with a bespoke agent, our open-source evaluation code runs any generic language model (LM) against them, and is secure against LM cheating attempts. We find that LMs equipped with a minimal set of tools resolve 147 of these conjectures with a budget of \$50 per attempt, scoring 30% on OEIS Open. OEIS Open Lite is a random subset of 100 conjectures for cheaper evaluation. When evaluated with a budget of \$200 per attempt, the best current LM scores 44% on OEIS Open Lite. Giving LMs access to the mathematics literature via 476,000 papers from arXiv did not increase performance on OEIS Open Lite, and nor did using more sophisticated agent loops. The conjectures covered in this work are of uncertain mathematical significance, and most have likely received little previous attention. Nevertheless, our results show that LMs can resolve open research conjectures autonomously and at modest cost.

</details>


### [244] [ExRole: From Team Trajectories to Executable Roles in Multi-Agent Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11949)
*Zhou Liu,Chaoyang Han,Zewei Pan,Zeli Su,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ExRole框架，将多智能体系统中的角色从手写提示标签转变为可执行控制变量，通过轨迹学习获得未来感知的角色原型，在MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA基准上显著优于单智能体和现有多智能体方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多个智能体系统将角色视为与学习行为分离的手写提示标签，缺乏与参数更新的关联。作者认为有用角色应该是可执行控制变量，能够总结预测未来效用的行为、指导后续交互，并识别负责该行为的可训练容量。

Method: 提出ExRole轨迹到角色框架：1) 从前缀局部团队轨迹中学习未来感知角色原型；2) 将原型解析为可读指令和标记对齐的角色标记；3) 可选路由共享LoRA秩槽位并赋予回合对齐信用；4) 通过Role-Agent-Turn干预分析验证角色的可转移行为专业化。

Result: 在MuSiQue基准上，ExRole相比单智能体搜索EM/F1提升15.0/14.4点；相比最强非ExRole控制方法保持11.5/11.6点提升。在2WikiMultiHopQA基准上，相比单智能体搜索EM/F1提升13.5/16.1点；相比最强控制方法保持7.7/9.7点提升。控制实验一致表明轨迹诱导的角色条件优于无角色、手动、随机和洗牌替代方案。

Conclusion: ExRole成功实现了从静态角色标签到动态可学习角色的范式转变。实验验证了轨迹诱导角色条件在多跳问答任务中的有效性，且诱导角色能够捕获超越固定智能体身份或回合位置的可转移行为专业化，为多智能体语言模型系统提供了更有效的角色建模方法。

Abstract: Roles provide an interpretable interface for organizing language-model agents, yet most multi-agent systems treat them as hand-written prompt labels disconnected from learned behavior and parameter updates. We argue that a useful role should instead be an executable control variable: it should summarize behavior predictive of future utility, guide subsequent interaction, and identify the trainable capacity responsible for that behavior. We introduce ExRole, a trajectory-to-role framework that learns future-aware role prototypes from prefix-local team traces, resolves them into readable instructions and token-aligned role markers, and optionally routes shared LoRA rank slots with turn-aligned credit. Across MuSiQue and 2WikiMultiHopQA, ExRole improves over single-agent search by 15.0/14.4 and 13.5/16.1 EM/F1 points, respectively. Against the strongest non-ExRole controls, the corresponding gains remain 11.5/11.6 and 7.7/9.7 points. Across both benchmarks, the controlled results consistently favor trajectory-induced role conditioning over role-free, manual, random, and shuffled alternatives. Role-Agent-Turn interventions further show that the induced roles capture transferable behavioral specialization beyond fixed agent identities or turn positions.

</details>


### [245] [Retry, Switch, or Abstain? Learning Strategy-Aware Tool-Use Policies via Controlled Error Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11977)
*Chaoran Chen,Vy Nguyen,Ziji Zhang,Abhinav Gullapalli,Ziyi Wang,Yuxuan Lu,Dakuo Wang,Jing Huang,Zhou Yu,Jin Lai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BENCH2ROBUST框架，用于评估LLM智能体在工具失败场景下的鲁棒性。研究发现跨模型存在普遍鲁棒性差距，贝叶斯工具记忆（BTM）和课程控制强化学习两种干预措施可显著提升鲁棒性，结合两者在攻击注入下达到40.8-45.5%性能同时保持无失败表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型LLM智能体通常在无失败环境中训练和评估，但部署时工具可能出现瞬态、持续或静默失败。鲁棒恢复需要智能体能够重试相同路径、切换替代方案或识别无可行路径的情况，而不仅仅是简单重试。

Method: 提出BENCH2ROBUST框架，将无失败工具使用基准转换为具有场景可控求解性的受控随机环境。研究两种互补干预措施：通过贝叶斯工具记忆（BTM）实现的结构化运行时恢复上下文，以及课程控制强化学习。在4个系列的7个模型和两个多轮基准家族上进行评估。

Result: 工具失败导致近乎普遍的鲁棒性差距。在保留零售任务中，BTM在不重新训练的情况下将鲁棒性提高最多16.8个百分点。强化学习学习到互补的恢复行为，在没有推理时BTM的情况下仍然有益。结合两种方法在注入攻击下达到40.8-45.5%性能，同时保持无失败表现。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的工具使用受益于将环境特定的恢复知识（通过BTM）与学习到的恢复行为（通过强化学习）相结合。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are commonly trained and evaluated in environments where tool calls succeed reliably, yet deployed tools can fail transiently, persistently, or silently. Robust recovery therefore requires more than repeated retries: an agent may need to retry the same path, switch to an alternative, or recognize that no viable path remains. We present BENCH2ROBUST, a framework that converts failure-free tool-use benchmarks into controlled stochastic environments with scenario-controlled solvability, where episodes explicitly require retrying, switching, or stopping after available paths are exhausted. We use BENCH2ROBUST to study two complementary interventions: structured runtime recovery context through Bayesian Tool Memory (BTM), and curriculum-controlled reinforcement learning. Across 7 models from 4 families and two multi-turn benchmark families, tool failures produce a near-universal robustness gap. On held-out Retail tasks, BTM improves robustness by up to 16.8 percentage points without retraining, while RL learns complementary recovery behavior that remains beneficial without inference-time BTM. Combining the two reaches 40.8-45.5% under injection while preserving failure-free performance. These results suggest that robust tool use benefits from combining environment-specific recovery knowledge with learned recovery behavior.

</details>


### [246] [Claim-Level Reliability Assessment for Efficient Test-Time Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11994)
*Sen Xu,Wei Wang,Shixi Liu,Jixin Min,Yingwei Dai,Zhibin Yin,Yirong Chen,Junlin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于声明级证伪的测试时扩展原则（CLR），通过有针对性的验证而非额外采样，利用语义证伪压缩错误轨迹生存空间，在多个LLM和基准上显著提升了推理可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整个轨迹评估常常因常规token的信号稀释而掩盖关键错误，且生成完全正确的解在固定模型能力下很困难，需要更有效的验证方法。

Method: CLR将每条推理轨迹压缩为一组决策关键声明，聚焦语义证伪，利用解构造与声明反驳之间的不对称性，通过寻找单个决定性错误来压缩错误轨迹的生存空间。

Result: 在四个LLM和四个推理基准上，CLR通常优于pass@1和自一致性。例如在GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25上，CLR比pass@1高27.15个百分点，并将自一致性准确率从77.50%提升到82.19%，同时减少37.0%的token。

Conclusion: 声明级证伪（CLR）是一种有效的测试时扩展策略，通过将额外计算转向有针对性的验证，能够显著提升推理模型的可靠性，并减少计算开销。

Abstract: We propose claim-level falsification as a principle for test-time scaling and instantiate it through Claim-Level Reliability Assessment (CLR), a training-free framework that reallocates test-time compute from additional solution sampling to targeted verification. Since whole-trace evaluation often obscures decisive errors due to signal dilution from routine tokens, CLR condenses each reasoning trace into a compact set of decision-critical claims, thereby isolating its logical anchors. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent difficulty of generating entirely correct solutions under fixed model capabilities, CLR shifts the focus to semantic falsification. This approach exploits a fundamental asymmetry between solution construction and claim refutation. Constructing a valid solution requires a flawless reasoning path, whereas refuting an incorrect claim requires identifying only a single decisive flaw. This targeted search for negative evidence systematically compresses the survival space of high-confidence incorrect traces, effectively suppressing erroneous consensus via nonlinear reliability scoring. Across four LLMs and four reasoning benchmarks under matched budgets, CLR generally improves upon pass@1 and self-consistency. On GPT-OSS-20B/CMIMC25, for instance, CLR exceeds pass@1 by 27.15 percentage-points and raises self-consistency accuracy from 77.50\% to 82.19\% with 37.0\% fewer tokens.

</details>


### [247] [CTBench: Evaluating Troubleshooting Capabilities of AI Agents in Realistic Telecom Network Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12002)
*Xingyu Yan,Tingting Dai,Antonio De Domenico,Mohamed Sana,Nicola Piovesan,Changchang Li,Bowen Liu,Kun Jiang,Mengjie Zhang,Dingcheng Shan,Jing-Cheng Pang,Chenwei Wu,Sijie Wu,Lianying Chao,Haoran Cai,Jiantao Ye,Xubin Li,Simon Mark Lucas,Xin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CTBench基准，用于评估电信故障排除智能体能力。实验发现当前先进智能体在路径恢复任务中表现良好，但在根因分析方面存在显著不足，特别是在提供运维实践所需的证据支持诊断方面。路径恢复资源消耗更高，但资源使用量增加并不必然带来更好的诊断效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体在网络运维自动化中应用日益广泛，工程师需要在严格约束下诊断网络故障、优化配置以提升服务并降低运营成本。然而，现有评估无法准确建模真实网络特征，也无法在具有多样化厂商、设备、协议和接口的部分可观察电信环境中有效评估智能体表现。

Method: 本文引入CTBench，一个公开基准，专注于根因分析和路径恢复任务。每个任务由专家构建，包含丰富的任务元数据标注，包括黄金证据步骤。CTBench采用专家基础的评估指标，同时评估最终答案和诊断证据的质量。

Result: 代表性格架-模型组合的实验表明：1）最先进的智能体在路径恢复任务中识别端点表现优异，但在根因分析方面普遍表现不佳；2）智能体在接口状态、链路层、服务管理及其他运维故障方面面临困难；3）即使智能体产生看似合理或正确的最终答案，也往往无法提供运维实践中所需的证据支持诊断；4）路径恢复通常资源消耗更大，但更大的资源使用并不必然转化为更好的诊断效果。

Conclusion: CTBench基准揭示了当前电信故障排除智能体的显著能力差距，特别是在证据支持的诊断推理方面。研究表明，现有智能体虽然能够在特定任务中取得良好结果，但缺乏运维实践中必需的系统性诊断能力，强调了在电信网络运维中部署智能体前需要进一步的技术发展。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly considered for automating network operations and maintenance, where engineers must diagnose network faults, optimize configurations to enhance services, and reduce operational costs while acting under strict constraints. However, existing evaluations fail to accurately model real network characteristics or assess agents under partially observable telecom environments with diverse vendors, devices, protocols, and interfaces. In this paper, we introduce CTBench, a public benchmark for assessing whether an agent behaves like a competent telecom troubleshooting engineer. CTBench focuses on root cause analysis and path restoration. Each task is constructed by experts and annotated with rich task metadata, including golden evidence steps. CTBench uses expert-grounded metrics that evaluate both final answers and the diagnostic evidence. Experiments with representative harness-model combinations show that state-of-the-art agents perform very well at identifying endpoints in path-restoration tasks but, more generally, underperform in root cause analysis. In particular, agents struggle with interface state, link-layer, service-management, and other operational faults. Most importantly, even when agents produce plausible or correct final answers, they often fail to provide the evidence-grounded diagnoses required in operational practice. Our results further show that path restoration is generally more resource expensive, yet larger resource usage does not necessarily translate into better diagnosis.

</details>


### [248] [Mechanist: AI as a Scientific Instrument for Discovering the Mechanisms of Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12036)
*Mengru Wang,Junfeng Fang,Shuofei Qiao,Zhenqian Xu,Haoming Xu,Haoxiong Wang,Shumin Deng,Linyi Yang,Zhixiang Cui,Xin Xu,Yunzhi Yao,Buqiang Xu,Fei Shen,Haozhe Luo,Yunxiang Wei,Ningyu Zhang,Julian McAuley,Tat Seng Chua,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mechanist系统——一个自主发现AI智能机制的智能体系统。通过构建包含13,000篇可解释性论文的知识图谱，整合4,300万篇跨学科论文数据库，以及32个基础机制分析方法，Mechanist能够比Claude Code等现有系统生成更有价值的机制假设并更可靠地执行实验。系统展示了从发现模型行为到解释和控制AI模型的递进能力，包括发现反直觉的安全风险、揭示信念机制理论，以及将机制洞察转化为实际干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型在各领域取得显著成功，但其能力机制和潜在风险仍难以理解。随着AI开发速度加快且越来越自动化，机制探索仍然主要依赖人工，这导致模型能力与我们理解和控制它们的能力之间的差距不断扩大。需要一种自动化方法弥合这一差距，实现AI智能机制的自主发现。

Method: 构建专注于可解释性的知识图谱（约13,000篇论文），并与涵盖26个领域的4,300万篇跨学科论文数据库集成。整理32个基础方法库，用于机制分析、因果干预和验证。开发智能体系统Mechanist，将AI作为科学仪器实现自主机制发现。系统与Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统进行比较评估。

Result: Mechanist能够生成比Claude Code和现有AI科学家系统更有价值的机制假设，并以更可靠的方式执行实验。系统展示了从发现模型行为到解释和控制AI模型的递进能力：1）发现科学实验室中的反直觉安全风险——不安全特征可通过看似安全的训练数据跨模态转移；2）发展信念机制理论——揭示模型如何表示世界知识、形成信念、推断他人信念，以及这些机制在预训练期间如何涌现；3）将机制洞察转化为实用干预措施，改善模型在不同场景下的性能，并引导科学基础模型生成具有指定属性的DNA序列。

Conclusion: Mechanist代表了AI机制自主发现的重要突破，证明了AI可以成为科学探索的工具。通过构建大规模知识图谱、整合跨学科资源和系统化方法库，该系统实现了从行为发现到机制解释再到实际控制的完整研究链条。这不仅有助于理解AI模型的能力和风险，还为改进模型性能和指导模型开发提供了新的方法论框架，为解决AI可解释性和安全性问题提供了有力工具。

Abstract: AI models have achieved remarkable success across diverse domains, yet the mechanisms underlying their capabilities and the risks they may pose remain poorly understood. As AI development becomes faster and increasingly automated, mechanistic exploration remains largely manual, widening the gap between what models can do and our ability to understand and control them. To bridge this gap, we introduce Mechanist, an agentic system that uses AI as a scientific instrument for the autonomous discovery of mechanisms underlying AI intelligence. To support autonomous mechanistic discovery, we construct an interpretability-focused knowledge graph of approximately 13,000 papers and integrate it with a multidisciplinary database of 43 million papers spanning 26 fields. We further curate a library of 32 foundational methods for mechanism analysis, causal intervention, and validation. Compared with Claude Code and existing AI-scientist systems, Mechanist generates more valuable mechanism hypotheses and executes experiments more reliably. Mechanist also demonstrates a progression from discovering model behaviors to explaining and controlling AI models. Specifically, Mechanist first uncovers a counterintuitive safety risk in scientific laboratories, showing that unsafe traits can transfer across modalities through apparently safe training data. Mechanist then develops a mechanism theory of belief, revealing how models represent world knowledge, form beliefs, infer the beliefs of others, and how these mechanisms emerge during pretraining. Finally, Mechanist translates these mechanistic insights into practical interventions that improve model performance across diverse scenarios and steer scientific foundation models toward generating DNA sequences with specified properties.

</details>


### [249] [Graph-Structured Rubrics: Compiling Rubrics into Typed Evaluation Graphs for LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12097)
*Xi Chen,Jie Mu,Mo Xuan,Qun Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出图结构评分标准（GSR），将评分标准编译为类型化的评估图，实现了更结构化和精确的评估，在多项基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分标准的评估器通常将评分标准视为提示上下文或扁平化的标准，使标准组合隐含，即使自然语言规则明确说明了组合方式。这种方法限制了评估的精确性和有效性，难以实现复杂的评估逻辑。

Method: GSR将评分标准编译为响应无关的类型化评估图，在观察响应之前完成。评估图中：标准节点触发判断；转换、归约和门控操作符通过命名端口进行组合；任务特定的输出映射（Readout）将唯一汇点转换为分数或偏好。编译过程会拒绝格式错误或类型不兼容的图。逐点评估分别判断评分标准维度再进行图聚合；成对评估在每条标准下对每个候选项使用一个判断来重用图结构。

Result: 在GPT-OSS-120B模型下，GSR在四个逐点数据集上比Prometheus式评分的精确分数一致性提高了0.62-6.75个百分点，并在两个偏好基准测试中在原生平局和弃权策略下达到了数值最高的端到端成对准确率。

Conclusion: GSR提供了一种更结构化、更精确的基于评分标准的评估方法，通过图编译和类型检查确保了评估逻辑的正确性，在逐点和成对评估任务中均展现出显著的性能提升，为AI模型评估提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluators commonly treat rubrics as prompt context or flat criteria: they specify what to judge but leave criterion composition implicit, even when natural-language rules state it. We introduce Graph-Structured Rubrics (GSR), which compiles a rubric into a response-independent typed evaluation graph before observing responses. Criterion nodes elicit judgments; transformation, reduction, and gating operators compose them through named ports; and a task-specific output mapping, termed Readout, converts the unique sink into a score or preference. Compilation rejects malformed or type-incompatible graphs. Pointwise evaluation judges rubric dimensions separately before graph aggregation; pairwise evaluation reuses the graph with one judgment for each candidate under every criterion. Under GPT-OSS-120B, GSR improves exact score agreement by 0.62--6.75 percentage points over Prometheus-style scoring on four pointwise datasets and achieves the numerically highest end-to-end pairwise accuracy on two preference benchmarks under native tie and abstention policies.

</details>


### [250] [Who Thinks Best Depends on How Long You Let Them: Budget-Dependent Rankings in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12150)
*Rodrigo Guedes de Souza,Alison R. Panisson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型在不同生成token预算下的排名并不稳定，3-19%的任务表现出非单调性行为，模型间排名会反转，表明需要采用基于预算的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的大语言模型评估假设模型在不同推理条件下的排名保持稳定，本研究挑战这一假设，通过改变token生成预算来检验模型排名的稳定性。

Method: 在7个预算水平(64-4,096 token)上评估4个模型在3个推理基准上的性能，共56,476次推理。分析非单调性行为、模型排名变化、oracle分析和预算感知的路由效果。

Result: 1) 3-19%的任务表现出非单调性行为(准确率随预算增加而降低)，且这种现象具有模型特异性；2) 所有基准测试中模型排名都会发生反转(p<0.01)；3) oracle分析显示模型互补性高达+27.8pp，在受限预算下最明显；4) 预算感知路由跨领域捕获oracle差距的14.1%，领域内有帮助(+1.6到+5.7pp)但不利于迁移(-1.2pp)。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持采用基于预算的评估协议，因为大语言模型的性能排名会随着推理条件(特别是token生成预算)的变化而显著改变，传统的固定预算评估方法不足以反映模型的真实能力。

Abstract: Standard evaluation of large language models assumes stable model rankings across inference conditions. We challenge this assumption by varying the token generation budget, i.e., the maximum tokens a model may produce, across seven levels (64--4,096), evaluating four models on three reasoning benchmarks (56,476 inferences). We report four findings: (i) 3--19% of items exhibit non-monotone behavior (accuracy decreasing with more budget), even after controlling for truncation, and this phenomenon is model-specific (cross-model overlap: 6--14%). (ii) Model rankings reverse across budgets on all benchmarks ($p {<} 0.01$, McNemar). (iii) Oracle analysis reveals model complementarity up to $+27.8$pp, most pronounced at constrained budgets. (iv) A budget-aware router captures 14.1% of the oracle gap cross-domain; budget features help within-domain ($+1.6$ to $+5.7$pp) but are domain-specific and hurt transfer ($-1.2$pp). These results argue for budget-conditioned evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [251] [GUIDE: Governed Unified Intelligence for Document-to-Artifact Generation in Enterprise Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12133)
*Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Mohammadreza Sediqin,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GUIDE框架，一个基于多智能体协同的企业指南文档处理系统，通过六个专业化智能体处理文档解析、VLM提取、一致性检查、评估、人工干预升级和定制化构件生成。在120份真实企业指南文档上验证，实现96%处理成功率，提取3896条规则，71.4%自动通过，生产812个部署就绪构件，处理时间从2-3天缩短至40-125分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档具有异质性和多模态特征，包含叙述文本、复杂表格和嵌入图像。现有LLM和VLM系统存在内容幻觉、表格结构退化问题，且缺乏超越提取的受控工作流（验证和构件生成），导致企业需进行手动处理，每份文档消耗2-3天时间。

Method: 构建GUIDE框架，一个受控多智能体系统，核心特征包括：共享版本化规则存储、Schema验证的智能体间契约、端到端溯源跟踪。六个专业化智能体分工协作：文档解析、VLM驱动提取、一致性检查、评估、人工干预升级、角色定制化构件合成。

Result: 在120份真实世界企业指南文档上评估，取得显著成果：文档处理成功率96%；提取3,896条规则，其中71.4%自动批准；生成812个部署就绪构件；单文档处理时间从2-3天大幅缩短至40-125分钟。

Conclusion: GUIDE框架有效解决了企业指南文档处理的效率和质量问题，通过多智能体协作和受控工作流设计，实现了高成功率、高效率的自动化处理，显著减少人工干预需求，为企业文档处理提供了可扩展、可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents are heterogeneous and multimodal, combining narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images. Existing LLM and VLM systems face hallucinated content, table structure degradation, and lack governed workflows extending beyond extraction to validation and artifact generation. This leaves enterprises to perform this manually, consuming 2-3 days per document. To address this, we introduce GUIDE, a governed multi-agent framework built on a shared versioned rule store with schema-validated inter-agent contracts and end-to-end provenance tracking. Six specialized agents handle parsing, VLM-driven extraction, consistency checking, evaluation, human-in-the-loop (HITL) escalation, and persona-tailored artifact synthesis. Evaluated on 120 real-world enterprise guideline documents, GUIDE achieves 96% document success, extracts 3,896 rules with 71.4% auto-approved, produces 812 deployment-ready artifacts, and reduces turnaround to 40-125 minutes per document.

</details>


### [252] [How to Spend Your Oracle Budget: Practical Guidance for Protein Structure Prediction Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12192)
*Aleksandra Kalisz,Jack Simons,Krisztina Sinkovics,Noam Ghenassia,Shikha Surana,Henry Moss,Paul Duckworth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了蛋白质结构预测中不同引导方法在有限Oracle预算下的性能，首次提供预算感知的实用指导。结果显示无单一方法在所有预算下均最优：O3在低预算时表现最佳，而FK-steering和DPO随预算增加效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前蛋白质结构预测模型对特定靶点可靠性不足，虽可用外部Oracle纠错但生物Oracle成本高昂，导致Oracle预算成为关键约束。现有引导方法（如FK-steering、DPO等）在预算分配策略上存在差异，但缺乏系统评估以指导方法选择。

Method: 通过基准测试比较四类引导方法：传统FK-steering、直接偏好优化(DPO)、Best K-of-N采样，以及扩展至蛋白质结构预测领域的优化输出法(O3)。实验聚焦两种靶标蛋白——钙调蛋白(1CLL)和大肠杆菌天冬氨酸转氨甲酰酶(9EEH)，模拟不同Oracle预算场景进行评估。

Result: ①无方法在所有预算条件下持续占优；②O3在低预算场景显著优于其他方法；③FK-steering与DPO性能随预算增加而提升；④方法有效性高度依赖具体靶标与Oracle类型组合。

Conclusion: 研究为Oracle预算受限的实际应用提供了首个系统性指导框架，并提出基于目标蛋白特性与可用预算的差异化方法选择策略，显著推动生物分子模拟领域资源优化问题的解决。

Abstract: Foundation models for protein structure prediction remain unreliable on certain targets. External oracles can flag and correct these failures, but biological oracles are expensive, making oracle budget a critical constraint. Existing guidance methods, such as FK-steering, DPO, and Best K-of-N sampling, differ in how they spend this budget, yet no systematic comparison exists to guide method selection. To bridge this gap, we benchmark these methods alongside the recently proposed Optimisation Over Outputs (O3), which applies off-the-shelf optimisers within a generative model's latent subspace. We extend the usage of O3 to protein structure prediction models. Overall, our work provides the first practical reference for oracle budget-aware guidance. Our evaluation on two protein targets, calmodulin (1CLL) and E. coli aspartate transcarbamoylase (9EEH), reveals that no single method consistently dominates across all budgets and oracles. Specifically, O3 proves most effective at low oracle budgets, while FK-steering and DPO demonstrate improved performance as the budget increases. We distil these findings into actionable recommendations for practitioners operating under real-world oracle-budget constraints.

</details>


### [253] [An Agentic Workflow for Legacy HPC Modernization: Converting the Two-Electron-Integral Core of GAMESS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12249)
*Yuzhong Shen,Masha Sosonkina,Peng Xu,Mark S. Gordon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合智能体工作流与精确验证机制的方案，成功将大型量子化学软件GAMESS中的遗留Fortran 77代码转换为Fortran 2008，在确保零实质性偏差的前提下实现了生产规模的代码现代化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留Fortran代码的现代化面临巨大的体量挑战：虽然单项转换是常规操作，但代码库极其庞大，导致计算科学领域的许多更新工作无法完成。

Method: 提出了一种智能体工作流（agentic workflow），由三个提示特化的智能体角色在版本控制的规范下协同操作，由人类保留少量关键控制点。安全性由领域内的精确验证预言机（exact verification oracle）保证，安全委派边界取决于该预言机的验证能力。具体应用于将量子化学软件GAMESS中计算双电子积分的12个源文件（共56,448行）从固定形式Fortran 77转换为自由形式Fortran 2008，以能量输出的逐位重现作为合并标准。

Result: 所有12个源文件均通过了包含51项测试的验证套件（涵盖49项GAMESS标准测试和2项额外计算），在612次测试运行中零化学相关差异，并通过了Jenkins持续集成测试。

Conclusion: 该方法在精确验证预言机定义的安全边界内实现了有效的任务委派，证明了基于智能体工作流能够处理生产规模的大型遗留代码现代化任务，且未引入实质性偏差。

Abstract: Modernizing legacy Fortran is a problem of volume: the transformations are individually routine, but the codebases can be enormous, and across much of computational science the work simply goes undone. We propose an agentic workflow that takes this work on at production scale, and we set out to measure how far such delegation can reach. In this work, three prompt-specialized agent roles operate under a version-controlled specification that the agents themselves authored and revised, while humans hold a small number of gates. The arrangement is kept safe by an exact verification oracle inherited from the domain, and the boundary of safe delegation lies exactly where that oracle stops seeing.
  We apply the proposed workflow in a case study, converting the two-electron-integral routines of GAMESS (General Atomic and Molecular Electronic Structure System), a mature quantum-chemistry package with a 48-year development history, from fixed-form Fortran 77 to free-form Fortran 2008. The scope of this work was twelve source files, 56,448 lines, and 225 subroutines for computing electron repulsion integrals. The agents ran as three Claude Code roles in isolated worktrees, and the work spanned four Claude model generations. Because the GAMESS group ships a standard test suite whose printed energies its user community treats as canonical, we could adopt bit-for-bit reproduction of those energies as the merge criterion, where a deviation in the twelfth decimal place counts as a failure rather than drift. All twelve source files pass a 51-test validation battery comprising the 49 standard GAMESS tests and two additional calculations, and across 612 test runs the number of chemistry-relevant differences is zero, and every file also passes the Jenkins tests that are used for continuous integration.

</details>


### [254] [VAKRA: Evaluating Multi-Hop Reasoning Across APIs and Retrieval Under Tool-Use Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12282)
*Ankita Rajaram Naik,Anupama Murthi,Benjamin Elder,Siyu Huo,Raavi Gupta,Abhinav Jain,Praveen Venkateswaran,Abdulhamid Adebayo,Danish Contractor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VAKRA基准，用于评估企业在结构化API和文档集合上的推理能力，发现模型在复杂场景下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准孤立评估企业代理的API和文档推理能力，无法反映真实场景需求。

Method: 构建包含8000+可执行API、62个领域的VAKRA基准，涵盖三种难度设置（多样化交互、多跳推理、多源策略约束）。通过重新执行预测的工具调用验证正确性，使用固定ReAct框架隔离架构影响。

Result: 最佳模型在单跳任务得70.4%，组合API任务降至50-51%，推理深度增加性能下降超50%，策略约束问题表现极差（低至2.4%）。分析显示失败主要集中在语言介导的推理。

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在处理复杂API和知识检索任务时存在严重缺陷，特别是在语言层面的推理能力（如实体消歧、跨源定位），而非工具调用机制本身。

Abstract: Agents deployed in enterprise settings must reason across structured APIs and document collections, yet existing benchmarks evaluate these capabilities in isolation. We introduce VAKRA (e\textbf{V}aluating \textbf{A}PI and \textbf{K}nowledge \textbf{R}etrieval \textbf{A}gents), a benchmark of over $8{,}000$ executable APIs across $62$ domains with tasks spanning three settings of increasing difficulty: diverse API interaction styles, multi-hop reasoning over structured APIs, and multi-source reasoning with natural-language tool-use policy constraints. Correctness is verified by re-executing predicted tool calls against live APIs, accommodating multiple valid paths. Using a fixed ReAct harness to isolate model capabilities from agent architecture, we evaluate frontier and open-weight models and find that even the best model achieves only 70.4\% on single-hop endpoint-style tasks and drops to 50--51\% on compositional APIs; performance degrades by over 50\% as reasoning depth increases, and policy-constrained questions expose severe failures (as low as 2.4\% on unanswerable queries). Trace analysis shows failures concentrate at language-mediated reasoning - entity disambiguation, cross-source grounding, rather than tool invocation mechanics. Code is available https://github.com/IBM/VAKRA. Dataset is available https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-research/VAKRA

</details>


### [255] [Constructing Dynamic Master Logic Models as Knowledge Graphs for Complex System Diagnostics Using Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12304)
*Saman Marandi,Yu-Shu Hu,Mohammad Modarres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成和大语言模型的自动构建动态主逻辑知识图谱（KG-DML）框架，将技术文档转换为可执行的功能模型，用于诊断和可靠性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态主逻辑（DML）虽然能有效连接系统功能目标与底层结构元素，但其构建通常依赖专家对技术文档的解读，难以扩展到复杂系统。因此需要一种自动化的DML构建方法，以提高可扩展性并支持大规模复杂系统的分析。

Method: 该研究开发了一个自动化框架，利用检索增强生成和大语言模型从系统描述中提取信息，构建DML模型并将其表示为知识图谱（KG-DML）。构建过程在DML层级中进行，通过定向检索保留功能依赖和显式逻辑关系。框架包括多层验证方法，评估各层的精度和召回率、逻辑门一致性和整体结构完整性。

Result: 将该框架应用于退役沸水反应堆的低压冷却剂注入系统，结果显示在不同运行中能够一致地重建模型。验证表明，自动化KG-DML构建可以将技术文档转换为可执行的功能模型，用于诊断和可靠性分析，并支持诊断推理、安全评估、向上故障传播和向下依赖追踪。

Conclusion: 自动化KG-DML构建框架成功克服了传统DML构建的局限性，实现了从技术文档到可执行功能模型的转换，为复杂系统的诊断和可靠性分析提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Dynamic Master Logic (DML) provides a hierarchical framework for representing system behavior by linking functional objectives to underlying structural elements. However, DML construction typically relies on expert interpretation of technical documentation, limiting scalability for complex systems. This study presents a framework for automated construction of DML models from system descriptions and their representation as Knowledge Graphs (KG-DML), using Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Large Language Models as enabling tools. Building on prior work with small-scale systems, the framework extends automated KG-DML construction and evaluation to substantially larger and more complex systems. Model construction proceeds across the DML hierarchy using targeted retrieval while preserving functional dependencies and explicit logical relationships. The resulting KG-DML supports diagnostic reasoning, safety assessment, upward failure propagation, and downward dependency tracing. A multi-level validation methodology evaluates layer-specific precision and recall, logical gate consistency, and overall structural integrity. Application to the Low-Pressure Coolant Injection system of a decommissioned Boiling Water Reactor demonstrates consistent reconstruction across repeated runs. The results show that automated KG-DML construction can transform technical documentation into executable functional models for diagnostic and reliability analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Self-evolving network verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11340)
*Ioannis Protogeros,Tibor Schneider,Laurent Vanbever*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动演化网络验证器模型的方法，通过编码代理从路由器软件中学习并扩展符号编码，解决了手工建模协议特征困难且维护成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号网络验证器虽能在海量路由输入和故障空间中推理正确性，但仅能验证专家手工编码的协议和特性。手工建模面临两大挑战：(1)厂商实现与RFC规范存在偏差；(2)行为随版本发布而变化。持续维护的负担阻碍了验证技术在许多网络中的应用。

Method: 提出让模型自动演化以忠实地捕捉实际网络行为。利用路由器软件作为权威规范来源，设计反例引导循环：编码代理向验证器的符号编码提议扩展，可信预言机（如模拟器路由器）提供真实路由状态作为基准，代理通过每次与预言机的分歧迭代优化网络模型。

Result: 原型系统成功教会3000行SMT验证器三个原本不支持的特性：OSPF区域、BGP路由反射、L3VPN over EVPN。系统自动收敛到与预言机一致的模型，甚至能发现厂商特定的行为差异。

Conclusion: 自动化模型增长将核心难题从编写验证系统转变为系统性测试验证系统。作者提出了关于信任和利用自动演化验证器的研究议程，为实现更广泛网络验证奠定基础。

Abstract: Symbolic network verifiers can reason about correctness across vast spaces of routing inputs and failures, but only for the protocols and features an expert has encoded by hand. Creating and maintaining a faithful model of the control plane is both difficult and never-ending, since no written source specifies perfectly what a network does: vendor implementations deviate from the RFCs, and behaviour shifts with releases. The burden of constant upkeep ultimately keeps verification out of many networks that need it.
  We argue that the model should instead evolve automatically to faithfully capture the actual network behaviour. To achieve that, we leverage the only source that specifies it unambiguously: the router software itself. In a counterexample-guided loop, a coding agent proposes extensions to the verifier's symbolic encoding, while a trusted oracle (e.g., emulated routers) supplies the ground-truth routing state. The agent iteratively refines the network model using each disagreement with the oracle.
  As early evidence, a prototype of this system taught a 3,000-line SMT-based verifier three features it did not support: OSPF areas, BGP route reflection, and L3VPN over EVPN, converging autonomously on models that match the oracle, even noticing vendor-specific behaviour. Automating model growth shifts the hard problem from writing verification systems to systematically testing them; we propose a research agenda for trusting and harnessing automatically evolved verifiers.

</details>


### [257] [Multi-Pair Fidelity-Aware Rate Allocation in a Quantum Network: Approximation Schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11501)
*Zunzheng Zhang,Xuanli Lin,Zhaofeng Zhang,Nageswara S. V. Rao,Guoliang Xue*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子网络中考虑链路保真度差异的多对速率分配问题，证明三个核心速率分配问题（速率和、最小速率约束下的速率和、最大最小公平性）均为NP难问题，并提出针对优化版本的全多项式时间近似方案（FPTAS），通过随机网络实验验证了算法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中的纠缠分发需同时应对有限的链路容量、概率性纠缠交换及异构链路保真度等挑战。现有工作仅处理所有链路保真度相同的特殊情况（可多项式求解），缺乏对实际复杂网络场景的有效建模方法。

Method: 构建三类速率分配问题模型；严格证明其NP-hard性；设计优化问题变体以在吞吐量/公平性约束下最大化端到端保真度；开发FPTAS算法求解优化问题。

Result: 1) 证明三个基础速率分配问题均为NP-hard；2) 提出FPTAS解决对应的优化问题；3) 实验表明所提方案在随机生成网络中具有计算效率优势。

Conclusion: 尽管速率分配问题存在计算复杂性障碍，但通过创新性的近似方案设计，可在保证保真度目标的同时实现高效资源分配，为量子网络工程部署提供理论支撑与实践路径。

Abstract: Entanglement distribution in quantum networks must jointly account for limited link capacities, probabilistic entanglement swapping, and heterogeneous link fidelities. In this paper, we study multi-pair fidelity-aware rate allocation in quantum networks. We formulate three rate-allocation problems: rate sum, rate sum subject to minimum-rate constraints, and max-min fairness. Prior work has studied a special case of the rate sum problem, where all links have identical fidelity. This special case admits a polynomial-time algorithm. We prove that all three problems are NP-hard. We then study optimization versions of these problems which maximize the minimum end-to-end fidelity subject to throughput or fairness requirements. We present fully polynomial-time approximation schemes (FPTAS) for solving these optimization problems. Experiments on randomly generated networks demonstrate the computational effectiveness of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [258] [In QKD, Key Metadata is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11502)
*Alin-Bogdan Popa,Pantelimon George Popescu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦量子密钥分发（QKD）网络中跨域密钥分发的语义互操作性问题，提出通过QKD密钥元数据作为通用语言，并给出开发该标准6步法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着联邦互联QKD网络成为常态，互操作性在技术上已可行，但跨域密钥分发中仍存在诸多未决问题，如违规公告与缓解、空段集成、QKD即服务商业模型、密钥新鲜度、司法管辖权、安全级别等自定义请求。为实现从语法到语义的互操作性转变，需要开发一种通用语言以确保与密钥一起传递含义。

Method: 提出使用QKD密钥元数据作为通用语言，并设计一个6步流程来开发该标准。

Result: 提出了QKD密钥元数据的6步标准化开发流程，旨在解决联邦QKD网络中的语义互操作性挑战。

Conclusion: QKD密钥元数据是实现联邦QKD网络中语义互操作性的关键，通过标准化开发流程可确保密钥分发过程中含义的准确传递。

Abstract: Federated interconnected QKD networks are becoming the norm, and with several standards already in place, interoperability is now technically feasible. Many aspects related to cross-domain key distribution, however, are left unresolved: breach announcement and mitigation, integration with the space segment, extension to a future QKD-as-a-Service commercial model, custom requests referring to key freshness, jurisdictions, security levels, etc. We argue that, to move from syntactic to semantic interoperability, a common language must be developed to ensure transfer of meaning along with the keys. We posit this is possible via QKD key metadata, and we propose a 6-step process to develop it as a standard.

</details>


### [259] [Integrated Sensing and Communication in 3GPP: Evolution from 5G-Advanced to 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11606)
*Xingqin Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了集成感知与通信(ISAC)在3GPP标准中从5G-Advanced到6G的演进，包括Release-19和Release-20的服务要求、信道模型、感知功能架构，以及6G原生感知技术，并分析标准化选择、实现权衡和开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC将移动网络从信息传输扩展至被动物体和环境感知，通过复用授权频谱、基础设施和边缘计算实现网络级检测和跟踪。与传统雷达不同，ISAC还需解决多应用接入、异构感知实体、不确定性、隐私、信任及与通信服务集成等挑战。

Method: 本文通过回顾3GPP标准演进，解析Release-19的ISAC服务要求和信道模型，阐述Release-20的感知功能架构和报告抽象，分析5G无人机检测跟踪的单静态基线，并考察6G原生感知的被动物体感知、通信辅助、多感知模式和多源数据集成等技术。

Result: 建立了连接5G-Advanced和6G阶段3GPP架构与无线研究的统一技术视角，系统梳理了当前标准化选择、实现权衡以及塑造网络级感知的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文通过对3GPP标准演进的深入分析，为理解ISAC技术的标准化进展、技术选型和实施挑战提供了综合框架，为网络级感知技术的发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) extends mobile networks from information transfer toward perception of passive objects and environments. By exploiting propagation delay, Doppler, angle, and temporal variation, a mobile network can support detection and tracking while reusing licensed spectrum, infrastructure, and edge computing at network scale and under operator control. Unlike conventional radar, ISAC must additionally address multi-application access, heterogeneous sensing entities, uncertainty, privacy, trust, and integration with communication services. This article reviews the evolution of ISAC in 3GPP from 5G-Advanced to 6G. It explains Release-19 ISAC service requirements and channel models, the Release-20 sensing function architecture and reporting abstractions, and the 5G monostatic baseline for drone detection and tracking. It also examines 6G-native sensing, including passive object sensing, communication assistance, multiple sensing modes, and multi-source data integration. By connecting the evolving 3GPP architecture and radio studies across 5G-Advanced and 6G, this article provides a unified technical perspective on current standardization choices, their implementation tradeoffs, and the open challenges shaping network-grade sensing.

</details>


### [260] [On the Allocation of Transmit Power for Coordinated Spatial Reuse in IEEE 802.11bn Multi-Access Point Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11971)
*Francesc Wilhelmi,Boris Bellalta*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于比例公平（PF）的框架，用于IEEE 802.11bn中协调空间复用（Co-SR）的每传输机会（TXOP）发射功率分配。证明了帕累托最优功率对至少有一个AP处于最大功率，将二维搜索简化为两个一维搜索，并在离散速率系统中得到闭式解。通过包级仿真验证，结果表明联合评估两个AP链路是关键，公平最优功率取决于速率是连续还是离散建模，且当前11bn信号支持自私策略但不支持公平最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEEE 802.11bn引入了协调空间复用（Co-SR），需要高效且公平的发射功率分配机制来支持多AP协调的同时传输，现有标准可能无法支持最优公平策略，因此需要新的框架。

Method: 提出一种PF驱动的框架，用于分配Co-SR发射功率。理论证明帕累托最优功率对至少有一个AP在最大功率，简化搜索复杂度。通过Kom8ndor仿真器进行包级仿真，与暴力搜索预言机和低复杂度自私基线进行比较。

Result: 结果显示：联合评估两个AP的链路是释放Co-SR空间复用增益的关键；协调AP的公平最优功率严重依赖于速率是连续建模还是通过离散MCS步骤建模；当前11bn信号支持自私策略但不支持公平最优策略，后者需要额外的每TXOP信道报告。

Conclusion: 本文提出的PF驱动框架简化了Co-SR功率分配，证明了公平最优策略需要额外的信令支持，且离散速率建模会影响最优功率分配。当前标准不足以支持公平最优策略，需增强信道报告机制。

Abstract: IEEE 802.11bn (11bn) introduces Coordinated Spatial Reuse (Co-SR), a Multi-AP Coordination (MAPC) scheme in which two Access Points (APs) coordinate to control the transmit power for a simultaneous transmission. This letter introduces a Proportional Fairness (PF)-driven framework for allocating Co-SR transmit power on a per-Transmission Opportunity (TXOP) basis. We prove that any Pareto-optimal power pair keeps at least one AP at its maximum power, reducing the joint two-dimensional search to two cheap one-dimensional line searches that fit comfortably within TXOP timing, and show that this collapses to an exact closed-form expression in a real, discrete-rate system. We validate the resulting policies, together with a low-complexity selfish baseline, against a brute-force oracle and through packet-level simulations in Kom8ndor, an 11bn network simulator. Results show that jointly evaluating both APs' links is key to unlocking Co-SR's spatial reuse gains, that the coordinated AP's fairness-optimal power depends critically on whether the rate is modeled continuously or through real, discrete Modulation and Coding Scheme (MCS) steps, and that current 11bn signaling supports the selfish policy but not the fairness-optimal ones, which would need additional per-TXOP channel reporting.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Extremely Large Beyond-Diagonal RIS: Low-Rank Modal Optimization for Near-Field Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11476)
*Giovanni Iacovelli,Chandan Kumar Sheemar,Eva Lagunas,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出超大BD-RIS概念，利用近场几何特性实现完全连接性能。通过球面波响应构建低维子空间，设计紧凑酉模态矩阵，使重构条目数量由几何决定且与面板尺寸无关，大幅降低硬件开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BD-RIS在全连接时性能最佳，但优化和硬件负担随元素数量呈二次增长（每次迭代三次增长）。超大表面使这种负担不可接受，同时其巨大孔径使基站和用户处于辐射近场区域，远场设计工具在此失效。需要可扩展的解决方案来处理极大规模表面。

Method: 1. 引入超大BD-RIS（XL-BD-RIS）概念
2. 使用自由空间格林函数对级联进行建模
3. 证明孔径场存在于由终端位置的球面波响应张成的低维子空间中
4. 在此子空间上设计仅基于定位信息的紧凑酉模态矩阵
5. 采用加权MMSE黎曼算法优化波束成形器和模态矩阵，具有单调收敛性和与面板尺寸无关的计算成本

Result: 24×24元素面板仅需约200个重构条目即可达到全连接最优性能，而传统方法需要30万个条目。不匹配的DFT波束空间因球面波前扩散导致60倍条目惩罚，经典分块架构在任何匹配条目预算下都提供严格较低的速率。重构条目数量由几何决定且与面板尺寸无关。

Conclusion: 近场几何正是使大规模全连接性能变得可负担的关键。本文提出的方法理论上可达全连接最优，同时显著降低硬件需求，在条目效率上优于DFT波束空间和分块架构。

Abstract: Beyond-diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (BD-RIS) achieve their best performance when fully connected, at the price of an optimization and hardware burden that grows quadratically, and per iteration cubically, with the number of elements. Extremely large surfaces make this burden prohibitive, while their sheer aperture places both the base station and the users in the radiative near field, where far-field design tools break down. This paper introduces the extremely large BD-RIS (XL-BD-RIS) concept and shows that near-field geometry is precisely what makes fully connected performance affordable at scale. Modeling the cascade with the free-space Green function, we prove that the aperture fields live in a low-dimensional subspace spanned by the spherical-wave responses of the terminal positions, and we design a compact unitary modal matrix on this subspace, built from localization information alone, that provably attains the fully connected optimum with a number of reconfigurable entries set by the geometry and independent of the panel size. A weighted-MMSE Riemannian algorithm optimizes the beamformers and the modal matrix with monotone convergence at panel-size-independent cost. Numerical results show that a $24\times24$-element panel reaches the fully connected optimum with about two hundred entries instead of three hundred thousand. A mismatched DFT beamspace pays a sixty-fold entry penalty rooted in the beam spread of spherical wavefronts, while the classical block-wise architecture delivers strictly lower rates at any matched entry budget.

</details>


### [262] [Small Language Model enabled Autonomous agent for Language-Conditioned Cognitive Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11596)
*Minhaj Uddin Ahmad,Zakia Zaman,Shunqiao Sun,Mizanur Rahman*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小语言模型的自主代理框架，用于实现语言条件化的认知雷达。该代理能根据自然语言指令自动选择、配置并调用雷达信号处理算法，在多种干扰抑制和波束成形任务中实现了有效的算法选择，且需要雷达专用提示和物理工具执行来确保决策可靠性和结果准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代雷达系统需要根据变化的干扰、杂波和数据可用性来调整处理策略，迫切需要一种智能自适应的处理机制。

Method: 设计了一个基于小语言模型（SLM）的自主代理框架，作为阵列信号处理工具套件的智能控制器。代理能够：1）从自然语言指令中提取雷达操作相关线索；2）选择适当的信号处理方法序列；3）配置参数；4）调用可执行工具进行数值计算。

Result: 在合成均匀线性阵列（ULA）雷达实验中，代理能够在旁瓣控制、干扰抑制、多零陷波束成形、相干源处理和低快照到达方向（DOA）估计等多种场景中实现有意义的算法选择。消融实验表明，雷达专用提示和基于物理的工具执行对于实现可靠决策和无幻觉的数值结果是必需的。

Conclusion: 提出的SLM驱动自主代理框架成功实现了语言条件化的认知雷达功能，证明了小语言模型能够有效驱动雷达信号处理算法的自主选择与配置。研究强调了雷达领域专用提示词设计和物理约束工具执行在确保系统可靠性和准确性方面的重要性，为智能雷达系统的自适应处理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Modern radar systems require adapting their processing strategies in response to changing interference, clutter, and data availability. This paper introduces a framework for a small language model (SLM)-driven autonomous agent designed for language-conditioned cognitive radar, functioning as an intelligent controller for a suite of array signal processing tools. Given a natural-language command, the agent extracts radar-operation-related cues, selects an appropriate sequence of signal-processing methods, configures parameters, and invokes executable tools for numerical computation. Experiments with a synthetic uniform linear array (ULA) radar demonstrate that, given a natural-language command, the agent performs meaningful algorithm selection across diverse scenarios for sidelobe control, jammer suppression, multiple-null beamforming, coherent-source handling, and low-snapshot direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation. Ablation results show that radar-specific prompting and physics-grounded tool execution are both required for reliable decisions and hallucination-free numerical results.

</details>


### [263] [Characterizing Nonlinearities in IM-DD Links via the Best Linear Approximation: Distortion Analysis and Modulation Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11754)
*Sebastian Fraga Fernández,Leonardo Minelli,Fernando de Bernardinis,Felipe Villenas,Yunus Can Gültekin,Alex Alvarado*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文采用最佳线性近似(BLA)对强度调制直接检测(IM-DD)光链路中的非线性特性进行建模，发现正交非线性失真呈现非高斯分布，并利用BLA为不同均衡器结构近似最优调制深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强度调制直接检测(IM-DD)光通信系统中，非线性效应严重限制了系统性能。准确表征非线性特性并优化调制深度对于提升传输质量至关重要。

Method: 采用最佳线性近似(BLA)方法对IM-DD链路中的非线性进行建模与分析。通过解析推导研究正交非线性失真的统计特性，并基于BLA为不同均衡器结构优化调制深度。

Result: 研究表明，IM-DD链路中的正交非线性失真呈现显著的非高斯分布特征。基于BLA得到的调制深度优化结果可为不同均衡器设计提供理论指导。

Conclusion: BLA为IM-DD光链路非线性表征和调制深度优化提供了有效的理论工具，所发现的非高斯失真特性有助于设计更优的信号处理算法。

Abstract: We use the best linear approximation (BLA) to characterize nonlinearities in IM-DD links. The orthogonal nonlinear distortion is shown to be non-Gaussian. The BLA is used to approximate the optimum modulation depth for different equalizer structures.

</details>


### [264] [RaStream: Edge-Deployable Streaming Human Mesh Recovery from mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11791)
*Jiazhen Dong,Lei Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RaStream是一个可在边缘部署的雷达张量流式网格恢复框架，通过雷达感知空间编码和双状态因果时间细化，在边缘设备上从毫米波雷达实现准确、平滑的人体网格重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达支持边缘应用的隐私保护人体感知，但现有方法面临稀疏雷达反射导致密集网格恢复困难、重型多尺度空间主干计算成本高且稀释微弱身体证据，以及逐帧估计存在抖动，因为通用时间模型混合了缓慢变化的身体形态与快速姿态和运动动态。

Method: RaStream结合了雷达感知空间结构(RaSS)编码器和双状态因果时间细化模块。RaSS编码器保留3D雷达结构，定位主体，提取身体中心证据，并从短雷达窗口生成紧凑的雷达感知令牌。双状态时间模块将慢形态状态与快速运动状态分离：通过令牌条件更新门累积形态证据用于形状和性别估计，并使用因果循环状态跟踪动态运动，保持流式内存固定，避免全体积缓冲。还提出了时间采样参数(Tw, T, s)以暴露雷达观测密度、有限展开范围、预热/回放行为及输出率权衡。

Result: 在M4Human数据集上，RaSS-Base将单窗口MVE从90.90 mm降低到84.27 mm，相比RT-Mesh参数更少。RaStream进一步将MVE降低到72.05 mm。Jetson Orin Nano性能分析显示Base配置FP32延迟为26.93 ms。

Conclusion: RaStream在基于雷达的流式网格恢复中实现了最先进的准确性和时间平滑性，同时在边缘部署方面高效，通过更低的MVE和在Jetson Orin Nano上的低延迟得到验证。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar enables privacy-preserving human sensing for edge applications, but streaming SMPL-X recovery on edge devices requires accurate spatial evidence extraction and temporally stable predictions under lightweight causal inference. Sparse radar reflections make dense mesh recovery difficult, and heavy multi-scale spatial backbones can be costly for volumetric radar tensors while still diluting weak body evidence with background clutter. Frame-wise mesh estimates further exhibit jitter, while generic temporal models often mix slowly varying body morphology with fast pose and translation dynamics. We present RaStream, an edge-deployable radar-tensor streaming mesh recovery framework that combines a radar-aware spatial encoder with dual-state causal temporal refinement. The Radar-aware Spatial Structure (RaSS) encoder preserves 3D radar structure, localizes the subject, extracts body-centered evidence, and produces compact radar-aware tokens from short radar windows. The dual-state temporal module separates slow morphology state from fast motion state: it accumulates morphology evidence for shape and gender estimation through a token-conditioned update gate and tracks dynamic motion with a causal recurrent state. The resulting model keeps streaming memory fixed and avoids full-volume buffering. We formulate temporal sampling parameters $(T_w, T, s)$ that expose radar observation density, finite unroll horizon, warm-up/replay behavior, and output-rate tradeoffs, and evaluate reconstruction accuracy, temporal smoothness, and edge efficiency on M4Human. RaSS-Base reduces single-window MVE from 90.90 mm to 84.27 mm over RT-Mesh with fewer parameters, while RaStream further reduces MVE to 72.05 mm under the random-split protocol. Jetson Orin Nano profiling shows 26.93 ms FP32 latency for the Base configuration.

</details>


### [265] [TRACE: A Modular Framework for RIS-Assisted Channel Estimation and Differential Channel-Aware Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11798)
*Smriti Kumar,Mansi Ambwani,Arzad Alam Kherani,Vimal Bhatia*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对可重构智能表面(RIS)研究中信道估计、控制和通信紧耦合的问题，提出了模块化实验框架TRACE和低开销的差分信道感知RIS更新算法DCAR。TRACE通过分离控制与数据平面接口实现各模块可替换，支持可重现评估；DCAR利用正则化差分更新从减少的探测观测中估计信道扰动，降低重新训练开销。实验验证表明，TRACE兼容多种信道估计方法、信道演化模型、调制方式和RIS尺寸，而DCAR在时变信道下能够减少导频开销和计算复杂度，同时保持接近卡尔曼滤波跟踪的波束成形性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RIS实现通常依赖固定算法流水线，导致难以在相同条件下比较不同的估计、跟踪和通信策略，也难以研究低开销的RIS如何适应时变信道。这种紧耦合限制了对RIS系统的灵活评估和优化。

Method: 本文提出两项贡献：(1) TRACE框架：基于套接字的模块化架构，通过独立控制平面和数据平面接口解耦发射机、无线环境、控制器和接收机，支持模块替换和可重现评估；(2) DCAR算法：差分信道感知RIS更新算法，利用正则化差分更新从减少的探测信号中估计信道扰动，以降低重新训练开销。

Result: TRACE框架经多种配置验证：可替换的最小均方误差(MMSE)和正交匹配追踪(OMP)信道估计模块、高斯随机游走和高斯-马尔可夫信道演化模型、BPSK/QPSK/16-QAM调制方式以及不同RIS尺寸，均无需修改底层框架。在TRACE中测试DCAR显示：相比基线方法，DCAR显著减少导频开销和计算复杂度，同时在时变信道下波束成形性能接近基于卡尔曼滤波的跟踪方法。

Conclusion: TRACE框架为RIS研究提供了灵活、可重现的实验平台，克服了固定流水线限制；DCAR算法实现了低开销的时变信道适应，在保持波束成形性能的同时降低系统开销。两项贡献共同推进了RIS系统的可评估性和实用性。

Abstract: Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface (RIS) research tightly couples channel-estimation, control and communication, yet existing implementations often rely on fixed algorithmic pipelines, making it difficult to compare alternative estimation, tracking and communication strategies under identical conditions and to study low-overhead RIS adaptation under time-varying channels. This paper addresses both challenges through two complementary contributions. First, it presents TRACE (Toolkit for RIS-assisted channel-estimation, adaptive control and communication experimentation), a modular socket-based framework that decouples the transmitter, radio environment, controller and receiver through separate control- and data-plane interfaces, enabling reproducible evaluation across substitutable modules. Second, it proposes the differential channel-aware RIS update (DCAR) algorithm, which estimates channel perturbations from reduced probe observations using a regularized differential update to reduce retraining overhead. TRACE is validated through interchangeable minimum mean square error and orthogonal matching pursuit channel-estimation, Gaussian random walk and Gauss--Markov channel evolution, BPSK, QPSK and 16-QAM modulation techniques and multiple RIS sizes, without modifying the underlying framework. Within TRACE, DCAR is observed to reduce pilot overhead and computational complexity while maintaining beamforming performance close to Kalman-filter-based tracking under time-varying channels.

</details>


### [266] [A Universal Random Precoding Framework for MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11828)
*Jiazhen Dong,Lei Liu,Xiaojun Yuan,Baoming Bai*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用随机预编码(RP)传输框架，通过随机变换利用所有子信道，构建属于通用性类的等效信道，在保持与现有波形向后兼容的同时提升分集增益，并在MIMO系统仿真中实现了接近最优性能的近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线系统通过对角化或稀疏化信道矩阵来对抗符号间干扰(ISI)，但仍易受选择性衰落影响，需要新的方法在保持兼容性的同时增强系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 提出基于通用性类的随机预编码(RP)传输框架，利用随机变换统计利用所有子信道并构建等效信道；2) 设计两种低复杂度实现：随机排列快速变换(FT-RP)和交织块稀疏快速变换(IBSFT-RP)；3) 为RP系统设计跨域OAMP/MAMP检测器(CD-OAMP/MAMP)，根据状态演进(SE)实现复制最大后验(MAP)最优性。

Result: MIMO系统仿真结果表明，RP结合CD-OAMP/MAMP检测器能够实现接近RM(Reference Method)的性能，同时具有更低的计算复杂度，并提供灵活的压缩比以优化频谱效率。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了选择性衰落问题，在增强分集增益的同时保持与现有系统的向后兼容性，通过低复杂度实现和灵活压缩比设计，为无线通信系统提供了性能与效率兼顾的解决方案。

Abstract: Current wireless systems combat inter-symbol interference (ISI) by diagonalizing or sparsifying the channel matrix, yet they remain vulnerable to selective fading. To address this, we propose a universal random precoding (RP) transmission framework based on the universality class. RP leverages random transforms to statistically exploit all subchannels and construct an equivalent channel belonging to the universality class, thereby enhancing diversity gain while maintaining backward compatibility with existing waveforms. Low-complexity implementations include the randomly permuted fast transform (FT-RP) and the interleaved block-sparse fast transform (IBSFT-RP). A cross-domain OAMP/MAMP (CD-OAMP/MAMP) detector is designed for RP systems, which is replica maximum \textit{a posteriori} (MAP)-optimal according to state evolution (SE). Simulation results on MIMO systems demonstrate that RP with CD-OAMP/MAMP achieves near-RM performance with much lower complexity, with additional benefits of flexible compression ratios for spectral efficiency.

</details>


### [267] [End-to-End Modelling of Earthquake-Induced Polarisation Perturbations in Submarine Optical Fibres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11903)
*Rick M. Butler,José Núñez-Kasaneva,Gabriele Liga,Magnus Karlsson,Alex Alvarado,Christian Häger*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端模型，用于模拟地震扰动下海底光纤中的信号传播。通过将地面位移转换为光纤应变，并结合分步模型进行模拟，该模型与2020年瓦哈卡地震期间Curie电缆的测量结果定性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要理解地震如何通过海底光纤影响信号传播，这对于地震监测和海底通信系统的设计至关重要。

Method: 建立一个端到端模型，将地面位移通过海洋压力转换为光纤应变，然后将其注入分步模型进行模拟。

Result: 模型与2020年瓦哈卡地震期间Curie电缆的测量结果在定性上吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该模型能够有效地模拟地震扰动下海底光纤中的信号传播，为进一步的研究和应用提供了基础。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end model of earthquake-perturbed signal propagation in submarine fibres. Ground displacements are converted to fibre strain via ocean pressure, and injected into a split-step model. Our model shows good qualitative alignment with measurements from the Curie cable during the 2020 Oaxaca earthquake.

</details>


### [268] [Disaster Information Reachability of Medium-Wave AM Broadcasting as a Wide-Area Disaster Information Infrastructure: Lessons from the 2024 Noto Peninsula Earthquake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11925)
*Eiichi Shoji*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了2024年能登半岛地震后，中波AM广播在灾害信息传递中的重要作用，发现该广播系统通过海上传播能够实现广泛的多方向接收，是重要的冗余灾害信息基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2024年能登半岛地震导致大范围停电、通信故障和道路中断，地震后立即发布海啸警报，突显了可靠灾害信息传递的重要性。能登半岛三面环海、交通网络有限，社区隔离和可靠信息传递的保障措施面临严峻挑战。

Method: 研究定义了"灾害信息可达性"概念，从该视角审视中波AM广播的作用。2024年9月，研究者使用商用收音机和被动收音接收设备（包括HOOPRA）进行了从金泽到珠洲市的移动接收调查以及珠洲市的定点接收调查。

Result: 新潟、富山、秋田和东京的中波电台接收清晰，而当地NHK金泽台受到噪声影响。全国大功率NHK第二广播电台网络（当时仍在运行）通过海上传播被确认提供了广域、多方向的接收途径，证明了其作为冗余灾害信息基础设施的关键意义。后续政策材料记录了FM转播站损坏情况，但中波AM广播未获得明确关注，表明其灾害信息可达性认识不足。

Conclusion: 中波AM广播凭借广域同时广播能力、大功率覆盖和海上传播特性，在半岛地区能有效发挥灾害信息基础设施功能，需要在南海海槽巨大地震等大规模灾害准备中重新评估其重要性。

Abstract: The 2024 Noto Peninsula Earthquake caused widespread power outages, communication failures, and road disruptions, and a tsunami warning was issued immediately after the earthquake, highlighting the importance of reliable disaster information transmission. The Noto Peninsula is surrounded by the sea on three sides and has limited transportation networks, making community isolation and securing reliable information transmission critical challenges. This study defines "Disaster Information Reachability" as the capacity to deliver necessary information to those who need it when they need it, and examines the role of medium-wave AM broadcasting from this perspective. In September 2024, the author conducted a mobile reception survey from Kanazawa to Suzu City and a fixed-point reception survey in Suzu City using commercially available radio receivers and passive radio receivers, including HOOPRA. Medium-wave stations in Niigata, Toyama, Akita, and Tokyo were clearly received, although the local NHK Kanazawa stations were affected by noise. The nationwide high-power NHK Radio 2 network, which was still operational at the time, was confirmed to provide a wide-area, multidirectional reception pathway through maritime propagation, demonstrating its critical significance as a redundant disaster information infrastructure. The fact that FM relay station damage was documented in subsequent policy materials, while medium-wave AM broadcasting received no explicit attention, suggests that its disaster information reachability remains insufficiently recognized. Medium-wave AM broadcasting, with its wide-area simultaneous broadcast capability, high-power coverage, and maritime propagation characteristics, functions effectively as a disaster information infrastructure in peninsular regions and warrants reassessment in preparation for large-scale disasters such as a Nankai Trough megaquake.

</details>


### [269] [Latent variable models for simultaneous EOV identification and removal in population-based SHM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11995)
*M. D. Champneys,M. R. Jones,A. J. Hughes,T. J. Rogers,E. J. Cross,K. Worden*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于缓慢性假设的贝叶斯状态空间高斯过程方法，用于处理PBSHM中未测量的环境操作变异性(EOV)，避免传统投影方法损伤敏感性丢失问题，在实验室和海上风电场仿真中均验证了其优越的损伤检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于群体的结构健康监测(PBSHM)中环境及操作变异性(EOV)的稳健处理是开放挑战，尤其在EOV信号未测量时。传统基于投影的方法通过丢弃健康特征数据的子空间来消除EOV，但当损伤作用方向与方差主导方向接近时，损伤敏感性会随EOV一并被移除，存在固有缺陷。

Method: 将潜在EOV建模为状态空间高斯过程，利用其长时间相关性（缓慢性假设）进行识别；通过卡尔曼滤波实现O(T)计算复杂度的推断；开发稳健的分层贝叶斯识别框架，使用拉普拉斯近似实现群体级别的潜在EOV和EOV无关残余特征识别。

Result: 在单个实验室规模基准结构上验证（热EOV），展示了稳健的损伤检测和EOV恢复能力；在模拟的九台风力机海上风电场（错峰部署和损伤）应用中，在相同假阳性率下，相比投影和协整基准方法实现显著的真阳性提升。

Conclusion: 论文提出的基于缓慢性假设和分层贝叶斯框架的潜在EOV识别方法，能够有效解决传统投影方法在损伤与EOV方差主导方向重叠时损伤敏感性丢失的问题，在实验室和海上风电场仿真中均验证了其优越性能。

Abstract: The robust treatment of environmental and operational variability (EOV) is an open challenge in population-based structural health monitoring (PBSHM). The difficulty is compounded in the case that the EOV signals are unmeasured. A common approach in conventional SHM is to apply \emph{projection-based} methods that discard subspaces of healthy feature data, reasoning that the EOV signal dominates the variance of the measured features. However, a common pitfall of projection-based approaches is that when damage acts close to the same variance-dominant direction, damage sensitivity is removed along with the EOV. An alternative identifying assumption for the removal of particular unmeasured EOVs is slowness; the latent EOV process is characterised by its long temporal correlation. In this paper, the latent EOV is cast as a state-space Gaussian process, enabling tractable $\mathcal{O}(T)$ inference via a Kalman filter. A robust hierarchical Bayesian identification framework is developed that enables population-level identification of latent EOVs and EOV-free residual features, using a Laplace approximation. The approach is first validated on a single laboratory-scale benchmark structure from the literature, subject to thermal EOVs, demonstrating robust damage detection and EOV recovery. The method is then applied to a simulated nine-turbine offshore wind farm with staggered deployment and damage, where it delivers a substantial true-positive uplift over projection and cointegration-based baselines at matched false-positive rates.

</details>


### [270] [Lightweight Single-Antenna Direction-of-Arrival Estimation for Curvilinear Trajectories in Mobile Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12029)
*Nour Zidi,Jérémie Voix,Pascal Giard,Lokman Sboui*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于接收机运动形成虚拟孔径的轻量级单天线框架，结合IMU航向和双向测距（TWR）进行到达方向（DOA）估计，验证了嵌入式范围域方位估计的可行性，并评估了相位相干MUSIC方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嘈杂的工业环境中，准确的到达方向（DOA）估计对于空间选择性通信具有重要价值，但现有的方法可能依赖于GPS、光学跟踪或高精度的外部移动接收机跟踪，限制了其应用。

Method: 采用一种轻量级单天线框架，利用接收机运动形成虚拟孔径。接收机使用机载惯性测量单元（IMU）航向和到已知固定信标的TWR测量。通过弯曲虚拟阵列MUSIC公式在任意轨迹上的相位相合窄带快照进行数值评估，并通过硬件实验验证使用修正和平均测距观测的范围域TWR-IMU方位估计器。

Result: 在100次连续四次旋转扫描的实验活动中，所有试验均保留。相位对齐累积恢复了123毫米的范围调制幅度，与测量的120毫米天线力臂高度一致。单次扫描方位精度为8.2°，绝对世界坐标系精度受限于电机化设置中BNO055磁力计的系统漂移。嵌入式方位估计计算每次估计消耗144.9 mJ，约占测试配置中测量循环能量的3%。

Conclusion: 结果确立了机载范围域方位估计的可行性，并推动了未来在自由形式移动轨迹期间对相位相干MUSIC方法的实验验证。

Abstract: Accurate direction-of-arrival (DOA) estimation is valuable for spatially selective communication in noisy industrial environments. This work investigates a lightweight single-antenna framework in which receiver motion forms a virtual aperture. The receiver uses onboard inertial measurement unit (IMU) headings and two-way-ranging (TWR) measurements to a known fixed beacon, avoiding GPS, optical tracking, and high-precision external tracking of the mobile receiver. The curvilinear virtual-array MUSIC formulation is evaluated numerically using phase-coherent narrowband snapshots over arbitrary trajectories. Hardware experiments validate a range- domain TWR-IMU bearing estimator using corrected and averaged ranging observations. In a campaign of 100 consecutive four-revolution sweeps, all trials are retained. Phase-aligned accumulation recovers a 123 mm range-modulation amplitude, in close agreement with the measured 120 mm antenna lever arm. The single-sweep bearing precision is 8.2° absolute world-frame accuracy is limited by systematic BNO055 magnetometer drift in the motorized setup. The embedded bearing-estimation computation consumes 144.9 mJ per estimate, approximately 3 % of the measured cycle energy in the tested configuration. These results establish the feasibility of onboard range-domain bearing estimation and motivate future experimental validation of phase-coherent MUSIC during free-form mobile trajectories.

</details>


### [271] [Hybrid STAR-RIS Architecture for Joint Localization, Communication, and Power Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12085)
*Haoran Ni,Mohammadali Mohammadi,Xidong Mu,Hien Quoc Ngo,Michail Matthaiou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合式星形同时透射与反射可重构智能表面（STAR-RIS）架构，通过动态切换有源与无源元件，实现定位、通信与无线能量传输（WPT）的联合功能。利用PARAFAC-ALS张量分解方法实现低开销信道获取，并在能效最大化问题上进行非凸优化求解，最终通过选择性激活少量STAR-RIS元件实现能效提升1.5至3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着第五代及未来移动通信系统对多功能集成需求的提升，如何在单一系统中高效实现定位、通信与无线能量传输的协同工作，同时优化系统能效，已成为关键研究课题。传统固定配置的RIS架构难以适应多场景需求，且全有源或全无源模式在能效方面存在局限，因此需要一种可动态调整元件状态的混合架构。

Method: 首先，采用基于交替最小二乘法（PARAFAC-ALS）的张量分解方法，将基站-智能表面与智能表面-用户信道解耦，实现低开销信道获取。在此基础上，构建以系统能效（EE）最大化为目标，并受通信用户频谱效率要求、感知信干噪比约束以及能量收集用户非线性能量收集需求约束的优化问题。针对该非凸问题中发射功率分配、STAR-RIS系数及有源/无源模式选择紧密耦合的挑战，提出交替优化两个子问题的方案，分别通过分数规划、逐次凸逼近及多种子贪婪策略初始化进行求解。

Result: 数值结果表明，相较于完全被动或完全主动架构，选择性激活少量精心挑选的STAR-RIS元件可在满足通信、感知与能量传输需求的前提下，将系统能效提升1.5至3倍，验证了混合架构在能效优化方面的显著优势。

Conclusion: 本文提出的混合STAR-RIS架构结合动态有源/无源元件切换，并通过PARAFAC-ALS张量分解实现低开销信道获取，以非凸优化方法求解能效最大化问题。研究证实，通过选择性激活部分元件而非全有源或全无源，可大幅提升系统能效，为未来多功能集成通信系统提供了一种高效、灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a hybrid simultaneously transmitting and reflecting reconfigurable intelligent surface (STAR-RIS) architecture with dynamically switched active and passive elements to support joint localization, communication, and wireless power transfer (WPT). We first pursue a parallel factor analysis with the alternating least squares (PARAFAC-ALS)-based tensor decomposition approach that decouples the base station (BS)-reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) and RIS-user channels, thereby enabling low-overhead channel acquisition. Based on this, we formulate a system energy efficiency (EE) maximization problem, subject to the spectral efficiency (SE) requirements of communication users, sensing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio constraints, and the nonlinear energy harvesting requirements of energy-harvesting users. The optimization problem is nonconvex since the transmit power allocation, STAR-RIS coefficients, and active/passive mode assignments are tightly coupled in both the objective and constraints. We address this issue by alternating between two subproblems, and solving them via fractional programming, successive convex approximation and a multi-seed greedy strategy employed as an initialization step. Numerical results demonstrate that selectively activating a small, well-chosen subset of STAR-RIS elements achieves 1.5 to 3 times EE improvements compared with fully passive/active architectures, while satisfying communication, sensing, and power-transfer requirements.

</details>


### [272] [Effects of Tool Wear on the Surface Texture in Turning: A Feature Characterization Approach Based on ISO 21920-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12163)
*Alexander Müller,Maximilian Berndt,Hagen Schmidt,Lars Müller,Matthias Eifler,Eberhard Kerscher,Benjamin Kirsch,Jörg Seewig*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究利用ISO 21920-2标准特征表征方法，对切削加工中刀具磨损引起的表面纹理变化进行空间分辨分析。通过构建包含12个AlTiN涂层硬质合金刀片在AISI 1045钢加工中9个磨损状态的轮廓数据库，结合分水岭分割和新型均值特征分解方法，发现磨损引起的纹理变化主要由确定性分量及其后刀面主导。沟槽级最大绝对梯度均值（R_dt_groove）单独即可解释83-90%的磨损指标方差，表明单一物理参数即可实现鲁棒的磨损评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表面粗糙度参数（如Ra、Rq）仅能全局描述表面形貌，无法空间分辨加工诱导的确定性结构。因此，本研究旨在评估ISO 21920-2标准化特征表征方法是否能提取刀具磨损信息并赋予物理可解释性，以实现对加工过程状态的实时监控。

Method: 研究构建了包含12个AlTiN涂层CNMG120408刀片在AISI 1045钢加工中9个磨损状态、每个状态3个重复轮廓的数据库。首先分析标准化场参数和特征参数与后刀面磨损、刀尖磨损及切削时间的关系。随后，改进分水岭分割算法提取旋转刀具沟槽并进行几何统计分析。进一步提出均值特征分解方法，将轮廓分解为确定性和随机性分量。最后，通过超过920,000种特征组合和多元模型评估，筛选关键特征。

Result: 磨损引起的变化几乎完全由确定性分量携带，且主要集中在切削沟槽的后刀面。与共聚焦测量对比证实，均值特征能重建参与切削的刀尖几何形状，后刀面陡峭化归因于第二切削刃的刻痕磨损。多变量模型显示，沟槽级最大绝对梯度均值（R_dt_groove）单独即可解释83-90%的磨损指标方差，证明单一物理参数足以实现鲁棒磨损估计。

Conclusion: ISO 21920-2特征表征方法能够有效提取刀具磨损信息，并提供物理可解释性。沟槽级最大绝对梯度均值（R_dt_groove）可作为单一鲁棒磨损指标，为后续基于散射光传感器的在线监控奠定了基础。

Abstract: The surface texture of a turned component acts as a fingerprint of both the process parameters and the tool wear condition, imaging the cutting edge. Classical surface parameters such as $R_\mathrm{a}$ or $R_\mathrm{q}$ describe the topography only globally and allow no spatially resolved evaluation of the process-induced deterministic structures. This work investigates how far the feature characterization standardized in ISO 21920-2 makes this wear information accessible and physically interpretable. The database comprises roughness profiles of twelve AlTiN-coated carbide inserts (CNMG120408) machining normalized AISI 1045 steel, measured at nine wear states over the entire tool life, with three replicate profiles per state. The correlation of standardized field and feature parameters with crater wear, flank wear, and cutting time is first examined. Watershed segmentation is then adapted to extract the rotational tool grooves and evaluate their geometry statistically. A newly developed mean-feature approach decomposes the profile into a deterministic and a stochastic component. Wear-induced changes are almost entirely carried by the deterministic component, and within it by the trailing flank of the cutting groove. A comparison with confocal measurements confirms that the mean feature reconstructs the engaged cutting edge geometry, with the trailing-flank steepening attributable to notch wear on the secondary cutting edge. An exhaustive evaluation of more than 920,000 feature characterization combinations and multivariate models reveals that the groove-level mean maximum absolute gradient $\overline{R_\mathrm{dt}}_\mathrm{groove}$ alone explains 83-90% of the variance of the wear indicators, so that a single, physically motivated parameter suffices for robust wear estimation. A follow-up study will investigate inline monitoring using scattered light sensors.

</details>


### [273] [Apnea Burden-Guided Framework: Enhancing Out-of-Distribution Generalization in PPG-Based Sleep Apnea Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12229)
*Mantas Rinkevičius,Oskar Pfeffer,Amal Alissa,Nando Hegemann,Vaidotas Marozas*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于睡眠呼吸暂停负担预测的新型框架，用于评估睡眠呼吸暂停严重程度。研究探索了PPG特征与SpO2结合对模型性能和分布外(OOD)泛化的影响。结果显示，结合PPG特征和SpO2在OOD测试中显著提升了所有性能指标，且低复杂度卷积-循环模型取得了最佳整体性能，展现出在家庭预防性监测中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸暂停是一种与显著心血管和代谢风险相关的常见睡眠呼吸障碍。虽然过夜多导睡眠图是诊断的金标准，但其复杂性和成本限制了其在家中长期预防性监测中的适用性。可穿戴系统中常用动脉血氧饱和度(SpO2)作为主要预测指标，但光体积描记(PPG)脉搏波的其他形态学特征通常未被充分探索。

Method: 研究提出了一种基于呼吸暂停负担预测的框架，用于睡眠呼吸暂停严重程度评估。该框架首先将呼吸暂停负担作为连续测量值进行预测，随后将其转换为临床相关的呼吸暂停-低通气指数(AHI)，用于在分布内(ID)和分布外(OOD)数据中将受试者分为四个严重程度组。研究评估了三种人工神经网络架构，性能指标在五次独立运行（使用不同固定随机种子）中取平均值。

Result: 在OOD测试期间，与仅使用SpO2相比，PPG特征与SpO2的结合使宏敏感性提高了约15.72%，宏准确性提高了9.22%，宏F1分数提高了16.01%，Cohen's kappa提高了11.08%，Matthews相关系数提高了13.22%。低复杂度卷积-循环模型取得了最高的整体性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，所提出的呼吸暂停负担指导框架结合PPG特征和SpO2，在独立外部数据集上展现出令人鼓舞的OOD性能，同时改善了睡眠呼吸暂停的表征。更简单的混合架构展示了在稳健的家庭预防性监测中的强大潜力。

Abstract: Sleep apnea is a common sleep-related breathing disorder associated with substantial cardiovascular and metabolic risk. Although overnight polysomnography remains the reference standard for diagnosis, its complexity and cost limit its suitability for long-term preventive monitoring at home. In wearable systems, arterial blood oxygen saturation (SpO2) is commonly used as the main predictor, whereas additional morphological features of the photoplethysmographic (PPG) pulse wave are usually underexplored. This study proposes a novel apnea burden prediction-based framework for sleep apnea severity assessment and investigates the influence of PPG features on model performance and out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization. The proposed framework first predicts apnea burden as a continuous measure, which is subsequently converted into the clinically relevant apnea-hypopnea index for subject-level classification into four severity groups in both in-distribution (ID) and OOD data. Three artificial neural network architectures were evaluated, and the performance metrics were averaged over five independent runs with different fixed random seeds. During OOD testing, the combination of PPG features and SpO2 led to increases of approximately 15.72% in macro-sensitivity, 9.22% in macro-accuracy, 16.01% in macro-F1-score, 11.08% in Cohen's kappa, and 13.22% in Matthews correlation coefficient, compared with using SpO2 alone. The low-complexity convolutional-recurrent models achieved the highest overall performance. The results indicated that the proposed apnea burden-guided framework, combined with PPG features and SpO2, improves sleep apnea characterization while showing encouraging OOD performance on an independent external dataset. Moreover, simpler hybrid architectures demonstrated strong potential for robust home-based preventive monitoring.

</details>
